@@@ 1 @@@
### no args: 
rc=64
--- stdout

--- stderr

gsdt-ui — the UI quality steps gsdt-flow-next asks for in a phase with screens.

Usage:
  gsdt-ui design                 create .planning/design/DESIGN.md (a stub to
                                fill with the user) and .planning/design/refs/
  gsdt-ui layout [<N>]           create <P>-LAYOUT.md (the layout contract) in
                                the phase folder; fill sketch: and pages:
  gsdt-ui check [<N>]            open every LAYOUT pages: entry at 375, 768 and
                                1440 px, measure it, take the pictures, write
                                <P>-UI-CHECK.md and the sheet (the app must be
                                running). Exit 0 passed, 1 failed, 2 could not run
  gsdt-ui check [<N>] --skip "<reason>"
                                record that the pages cannot be checked
  gsdt-ui look [<N>]             create <P>-UI-LOOK.md: one line to answer per
                                picture and layout rule
  gsdt-ui approve [<N>]          record the user's yes in <P>-UI-APPROVAL.md
  gsdt-ui approve [<N>] --waive "<reason>"
                                record that the user chose not to look
  gsdt-ui sheet [<N>]            print the path of the sheet (<P>-SHOTS/index.html)
  gsdt-ui shots [<N>]            pictures only, with the Playwright CLI (no
                                checks, no Node script); --skip "<reason>"
  gsdt-ui status [<N>]           design / layout / check / look / approval state

  <N>       phase number; default: from the current phase-<N>-* branch
  --repo    checkout to work in (default: the current one)

design, layout and look never overwrite answers; check replaces the last
pictures and report.

The checks (each line of <P>-UI-CHECK.md names one):
  C1  the page scrolls sideways            C5  an <img> did not load
  C2  text may be cut off (a question)     C6  an uncaught page error
  C3  contrast (axe-core, WCAG AA)         C7  the main button is missing,
  C4  text under ui_min_font (12px)            below the first screen or covered
                                           C8  the page did not load, or is
                                               another page (a login)
In <P>-LAYOUT.md: `main_button: /path = Text` (or css=<selector>, or none),
`expect: /path = text on the page`, `app: <folder with Playwright>`, and
`waive: <check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>` for a finding the
user accepts.

The app address, first found: `url:` in <P>-LAYOUT.md (this phase's app —
for projects with several apps), `ui_url` in .gsd.conf, then
http://localhost:{port}.
  {port}       the port gsdt-derive-port gives this phase, from the base port
               in a package.json dev script (gsdt-derive-port.sh <base>)
  {port:5173}  the same, with the base port written here
A fixed address (no {port}) works too, but then parallel phases share it.

check needs Node and a Playwright package: GSD_PLAYWRIGHT_ROOT, `app:` in
LAYOUT, the repo root, apps/* and packages/*, then the global one. Install
once per machine (projects without their own then share it):
  npm i -g playwright && npx playwright install chromium
It runs the project's Playwright with your rights, like the project's tests.
shots needs only the Playwright CLI (GSD_PLAYWRIGHT overrides).

ui_gates in .gsd.conf: warn (default), strict (the guard and gsdt-finish
enforce), off.

--- file .gsd.conf
base = develop
install = none

--- file .planning/

--- file .planning/ROADMAP.md
# Roadmap

### Phase 5: Shop

--- file .planning/phases/

--- file .planning/phases/05-shop/

--- file .planning/phases/05.1-patch/

--- file .planning/phases/05.1-patch/.keep

--- file package.json
{ "scripts": { "dev": "PORT=$(bash ../../scripts/gsdt-derive-port.sh <BASE>) next dev" } }

# Design system

<!-- gsd:design-stub — delete this line once the sections below are filled -->

Every screen in this project uses what is listed here. Don't invent new
styles: add to this file first, then use it.

## References

3–5 screenshots of real apps whose look you want, in `.planning/design/refs/`
(Mobbin, Refero, or apps you use). One line each: what to take from it.

Start from a real design system, not a blank page: ask the agent to browse
https://styles.refero.design (real systems written for AI: colors, type,
spacing) and pick the 3 closest to this product; the user chooses one, or
which parts of each to mix. Name it here.

## Tokens

Color, type scale, spacing, radius, shadow — and the file they live in.

Fonts: pick them on purpose — the default font is the first sign of an
AI-made page. Free: https://www.fontshare.com; pairs for headings and body:
https://fontjoy.com.

## Components

Name → file (seed from shadcn/Radix or the project's UI kit). Take a tested
part before drawing one: https://ui.shadcn.com, https://21st.dev,
https://reactbits.dev (React). Icons from one set only.

## Page templates

List, detail, form, dashboard, … → file. Each phase names the template its
screens use in `<P>-LAYOUT.md`.

## Layout rules

Every picture of a built page is held against these (`gsdt-ui look`). Edit
them for this project; keep the `- R<n>: ` form.

- R1: One main focus per screen
- R2: The main button is on the first screen, never covered
- R3: More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
- R4: One alignment system per page
- R5: Consistent title casing
- R6: Header and footer are lighter than the content
- R7: No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
- R8: Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
- R9: Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
- R10: Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

--- file .planning/design/refs/

--- file .planning/design/refs/.gitkeep

--- file .planning/phases/

--- file .planning/phases/05-shop/

--- file .planning/phases/05.1-patch/

--- file .planning/phases/05.1-patch/.keep

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.
     - url (optional): the app to open when the project has several, e.g.
       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.

     - waive (only with the user's yes), one line per accepted finding:
       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.
     - url (optional): the app to open when the project has several, e.g.
       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.
     - main_button: one line per page — "/path = Button text", or
       "/path = css=<selector>", or "/path = none". Without "/path =" the
       line holds for every page.

     - waive (only with the user's yes), one line per accepted finding:
       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: 
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: 
  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.
     - url (optional): the app to open when the project has several, e.g.
       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.
     - main_button: one line per page — "/path = Button text", or

     - waive (only with the user's yes), one line per accepted finding:
       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: fixed — the title wraps now
  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.
     - main_button: one line per page — "/path = Button text", or
       "/path = css=<selector>", or "/path = none". Without "/path =" the
       line holds for every page.
     - expect (optional): "/path = text that must be visible on that page".
     - waive (only with the user's yes), one line per accepted finding:
       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: fixed — the title wraps now
  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.
     - url (optional): the app to open when the project has several, e.g.
       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.
     - main_button: one line per page — "/path = Button text", or
       "/path = css=<selector>", or "/path = none". Without "/path =" the
       line holds for every page.
     - expect (optional): "/path = text that must be visible on that page".
     - waive (only with the user's yes), one line per accepted finding:
       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: fixed — the title wraps now
  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: fixed — the title wraps now
  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.
     - url (optional): the app to open when the project has several, e.g.
       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.

     - waive (only with the user's yes), one line per accepted finding:
       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

- R1: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R2: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R3: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R4: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R5: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R6: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R7: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R8: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R9: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- R10: ok — looks right in the picture
  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: fixed — the title wraps now
  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.
     - url (optional): the app to open when the project has several, e.g.
       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.

       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

## / at 375px

picture: 05-SHOTS/home-375-aaaaaa.png

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture

  was: ok — looks right in the picture
- Q1: fixed — the title wraps now
  was: bad — the title is cut off
  asks (C2): possible clipped text in 1 element(s): div.box "long | text"

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

# Phase 5 UI look

Open each picture below and answer every line after its colon with one of:

    ok — <what you saw>          fixed — <what was wrong, what changed>
    bad — <what is wrong>        n/a — <why the rule does not apply>

An answer needs its text. `bad` keeps the step open: fix the page, run
`gsdt-ui check 5` again, then `gsdt-ui look 5`. Keep the `## ` and `- R1:` parts as they are.

## Rules

R1 — One main focus per screen
R2 — The main button is on the first screen, never covered
R3 — More than ~12 items → search, filters or tabs
R4 — One alignment system per page
R5 — Consistent title casing
R6 — Header and footer are lighter than the content
R7 — No large empty areas; cards in a row have equal structure
R8 — Images: same shape and treatment; no blank or near-empty images
R9 — Nothing wraps alone onto its own row at any size
R10 — Matches the chosen sketch: same sections, same order

     reads sketch: and pages:; gsdt-ui check reads the rest.
     - url (optional): the app to open when the project has several, e.g.
       http://localhost:{port:3100} ({port:<base>} = this phase's port).
     - app (optional): the folder where Playwright is installed, e.g. apps/web.
     - main_button: one line per page — "/path = Button text", or
       "/path = css=<selector>", or "/path = none". Without "/path =" the
       line holds for every page.
     - expect (optional): "/path = text that must be visible on that page".
     - waive (only with the user's yes), one line per accepted finding:
       "<check> | <page> | <width> | <target> | <reason>"  (* = any). -->
